- **decision-capture**: Use when a change involves a trade-off, when choosing between options or rejecting an alternative, when the user says "we decided", "let's go with", "record why" or "write an ADR", or at PR time when a diff encodes a choice that is not written down. Writes a one-screen decision record (docs/decisions/NNNN-title.md, or the repo's existing ADR folder) with context, options, rejected alternatives, assumptions and what would make it wrong, and links it to the commit with a "Decision:" trailer. Optional commit-msg hook checks the link. ([squarepegg/agent-toolkit](https://github.com/squarepegg/agent-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/regen/decision-capture))
- **decision-card**: Use when an errand needs a real choice — present exactly two options plus a recommended default. ([Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates](https://github.com/Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates/tree/HEAD/templates/community/inbox-calendar/done/skills/decision-card) · ★6)
- **decision-card**: Pressure-test a decision before committing to it. Use ONLY when explicitly invoked — "reflect on &lt;decision&gt;", "run a decision card on &lt;x&gt;", "should I X or Y", "help me decide between", "stress-test this call". Runs a short loop: size the undo cost, look for a cheaper third option, attack the answer, then deliver an answer-first decision card with a real confidence number and the case against. Does NOT fire itself; never runs unprompted; never fetches URLs, edits global config, or installs anything. ([andrewluxem/decision-card](https://github.com/andrewluxem/decision-card/tree/HEAD/skills/decision-card) · ★1)
- **decision-card**: Present a choice the owner must make as one card per option (user experience before/after, code snippet, complexity estimate, rejection reason), then offer the pick through AskUserQuestion. Use when the owner must choose between options and the choice needs its experience, code and cost shown, from plain chat or from another skill (plan-create-overview, plan-create-implementation, next-commit), or when the owner asks for "before/after, code snippet and complexity" on an open question. ([0styx0/course-merchant](https://github.com/0styx0/course-merchant/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/decision-card))
- **decision-card**: Formats any choice for Derek as a short Decision Card (options, pros, cons, effort, recommendation) and then stops to wait for his answer. Use whenever there is more than one reasonable way forward, a do-it-or-skip-it choice, a new dependency or tool to add or a "what should we work on next" moment. ([derekburnz/fairshuffle](https://github.com/derekburnz/fairshuffle/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/decision-card))
